# OJS 3.5.0-5 — Field-level table mapping

Generated from the official OJS tag and its exact `pkp-lib` gitlink. This is an import coverage matrix, not a claim that every legacy table exists in a fresh 3.5 database.

- OJS commit: `372e3b84740344db6c2f85193c3eb3e4f539cb00`
- pkp-lib commit: `8b5f0fdc8d5664000b8652002781a14bd406bf21`
- Coverage: **134/134 source table names**; **132 fresh/plugin tables** and **2 legacy-source-only tables**.
- Fresh core/PKP install migrations create 130 tables; bundled `generic/staticPages` creates 2 more.
- `institutional_subscription_ip` is removed by `classes/migration/upgrade/v3_4_0/I6895_Institutions.php`; `submission_tombstones` is superseded by generic data-object tombstones. Both remain covered for version-aware legacy imports.
- Target persistence decision: reuse existing entities in their current schemas through the main `HibernateUtil`/`hibernate.cfg.xml`; place only genuinely new journal-specific tables in `penelitiandanpengabdian`. Source rows use external JDBC read-only, not `OjsHibernateUtil`. BLOB content reuses `LampiranJurnal` on the streaming SessionFactory keyed by scalar `RepoBitstream.id`.
- Existing-first RBAC decision: OJS role groups map to the active AIS `Tbmrole`, versioned `jurnalAksesJson`, scoped `PenugasanTahapJurnal`, and provenance. No parallel global journal role/group table is introduced.
- Existing-first publication decision: `JurnalPenelitian`, the Repository AIS aggregate (`RepoCollection`, `RepoItem`, `RepoItemMetadata`, `RepoBitstream`, contributor/event/usage models), `Artikel`, notification/payment/institution models, and `LampiranJurnal` are reused before any journal table is introduced. The 134 source tables are coverage, not a target-table count.
- Final table budget: 11 source tables remain `NEW_MODEL` and consolidate into six domain tables (`TemplateEmailJurnal`, `LanggananJurnal`, `UndanganPeranJurnal`, `PesertaDiskusiJurnal`, `PenugasanTahapJurnal`, `PenugasanReviewerJurnal`). Together with two support and four import-control tables, the implementation target is 12 new tables.
- Review/subscription consolidation: subscription catalogs use versioned `RepoCollection.accessPolicyJson`; review-form definitions use versioned `RepoCollection.workflowProfileJson`; round lifecycle uses `RepoWorkflowEvent`; each subscription/reviewer assignment stores its immutable policy/response snapshot.
- Generated by `docs/jurnal/Generate-Ojs3505TableMapping.ps1`; generation fails on commit mismatch, duplicate/missing table names, or missing schema definitions.

## Decision vocabulary

`EXISTING_VERIFIED`, `ALTER_EXISTING`, `NEW_MODEL`, `MERGED`, `DERIVED`, and `NOT_APPLICABLE_WITH_RATIONALE` have the meanings required by the implementation specification. No model implementation may start until this generated matrix and the candidate audit are reviewed together.

## `announcement_settings`

- **Presence:** `FRESH_3505`
- **Authoritative source:** `lib/pkp/classes/migration/install/AnnouncementsMigration.php`
- **AIS candidate:** src/ais/database/model/penelitiandanpengabdian/PengumumanPenelitian.java; src/ais/database/model/KategoriPengumuman.java; src/ais/database/model/file/LampiranPengumumanPenelitian.java
- **Decision:** `ALTER_EXISTING`
- **Target class/table:** PengumumanPenelitian plus KategoriPengumuman/LampiranPengumumanPenelitian and provenance
- **Target persistence:** entity existing tetap pada schema existing-nya dan seluruh tabel journal-specific baru berada di `penelitiandanpengabdian`, semuanya melalui `HibernateUtil` + `hibernate.cfg.xml`. Source dibaca JDBC read-only. BLOB file/galley memakai `LampiranJurnal` pada SessionFactory streaming dengan scalar `RepoBitstream.id`, tanpa ORM lintas database.
- **Semantics/decision rationale:** Add nullable JurnalPenelitian scope and localized metadata JSON to the existing research announcement path; do not create PengumumanJurnal/JenisPengumumanJurnal tables.
- **Conflict policy:** provenance key `(sourceInstanceUuid, sourceTable, sourcePrimaryKey, sourceRevision)`; normalized DOI/URN/email/slug conflicts require explicit link, non-destructive merge, external profile, skip, or manual resolution. Never match people by name alone.
- **Transform:** preserve locale and raw source value in provenance; normalize identifiers, UTC/date semantics, booleans/enums, safe HTML, and scalar cross-database file references in a version-aware adapter.
- **Test:** `MAP-001` verifies row counts, every source column, relationship counts, collision behavior, and dry-run/write reconciliation.

| Source column | Type / size | Nullable | Default | Source modifiers | Field-level target mapping |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---|
| `announcement_setting_id` | `bigIncrements` | NO | `` | `` | ImportMappingOjs.rawPayload.announcementSettingId; allowlisted key is normalized into PengumumanPenelitian plus KategoriPengumuman/LampiranPengumumanPenelitian and provenance configuration/metadata JSON; unrecognized key remains provenance only |
| `announcement_id` | `bigInteger` | NO | `` | `` | ImportMappingOjs.rawPayload.announcementId; allowlisted key is normalized into PengumumanPenelitian plus KategoriPengumuman/LampiranPengumumanPenelitian and provenance configuration/metadata JSON; unrecognized key remains provenance only |
| `locale` | `string, 28` | NO | `''` | `->default('')` | ImportMappingOjs.rawPayload.locale; allowlisted key is normalized into PengumumanPenelitian plus KategoriPengumuman/LampiranPengumumanPenelitian and provenance configuration/metadata JSON; unrecognized key remains provenance only |
| `setting_name` | `string, 255` | NO | `` | `` | ImportMappingOjs.rawPayload.settingName; allowlisted key is normalized into PengumumanPenelitian plus KategoriPengumuman/LampiranPengumumanPenelitian and provenance configuration/metadata JSON; unrecognized key remains provenance only |
| `setting_value` | `mediumText` | YES | `` | `->nullable()` | ImportMappingOjs.rawPayload.settingValue; allowlisted key is normalized into PengumumanPenelitian plus KategoriPengumuman/LampiranPengumumanPenelitian and provenance configuration/metadata JSON; unrecognized key remains provenance only |

**Indexes, unique constraints, and foreign keys:** ``foreign('announcement_id')->references('announcement_id')->on('announcements')->onDelete('cascade')``; ``index(['announcement_id'], 'announcement_settings_announcement_id')``; ``unique(['announcement_id', 'locale', 'setting_name'], 'announcement_settings_unique')``
